    EnumDef: EnumDef
    FieldAccess: FieldAccessData
    FieldAssign: FieldAssignData
    Return
    If
    While
    ForIn: ForInData
    NamedArg: Str
    Break
    Continue
    Switch
    Match: MatchData
    Case: CaseData
    NoDefaultArg
    Throw
    Catch
    RestPattern
    CaptureBlock: CaptureBlockData
    Loc
    BodyValue: CaptureBlockData
    ListPattern
namespace:
    func is_literal_str(self: NodeType) returns shallow Bool
    func is_literal_num(self: NodeType) returns shallow Bool

** [[file:src/std/file.org::#til.src.std.file.File][File]]
#+begin_src til
struct File {
    ref mut handle : U8
    mut is_write : Bool
    mut is_update : Bool
namespace:
    proc readfile(path: Str) returns Str
    proc writefile(path: Str, content: Str)
    proc appendfile(path: Str, content: Str)
    proc remove(path: Str)
    proc exists(path: Str) returns shallow Bool
    proc is_dir(path: Str) returns shallow Bool
    proc is_file(path: Str) returns shallow Bool
    func is_absolute(path: Str) returns shallow Bool
    proc remove_tree(path: Str)
    proc rename(src: Str, dst: Str)
    proc new(path: Str, is_write: Bool) returns File
    proc update(path: Str) returns File
    proc append(path: Str) returns File
    proc write(self: File, s: Str) returns ref File
    proc read_all(self: File) returns Str
    proc read(self: File, shallow count: I64) returns Str
    proc tell(self: File) returns shallow I64
    proc seek(self: File, pos: I64) returns ref File
    proc seek_cur(self: File, delta: I64) returns ref File
    proc seek_end(self: File, delta: I64) returns ref File
    func close(self: File)
    func delete(own self: File, shallow call_free: Bool)
    proc fwrite(self: File, parts: ..Str) returns ref File
    func size() returns shallow USize
    func clone(self: File) returns File
}
#+end_src

** [[file:src/std/sys.org::#til.src.std.sys.Cmd][Cmd]]
#+begin_src til
struct Cmd {
    ref mut handle : U8
namespace:
    proc new(args: ..Str) returns Cmd
    proc arg(self: Cmd, a: Str) returns ref Cmd
    proc args(self: Cmd, more: ..Str) returns ref Cmd
    proc cwd(self: Cmd, dir: Str) returns ref Cmd
    proc env(self: Cmd, name: Str, value: Str) returns ref Cmd
    proc stdout_to(self: Cmd, path: Str) returns ref Cmd
    proc stderr_to(self: Cmd, path: Str) returns ref Cmd
    proc stderr_to_stdout(self: Cmd) returns ref Cmd
    proc capture(self: Cmd) returns ref Cmd
    proc capture_out(self: Cmd) returns ref Cmd
    proc render(self: Cmd) returns Str
    proc start(self: Cmd) returns shallow I64
    proc wait(self: Cmd) returns shallow I64
    proc poll(self: Cmd) returns shallow Bool
    proc status(self: Cmd) returns shallow I64
    proc run(self: Cmd) returns shallow I64
    proc run_checked(self: Cmd)
    proc out(self: Cmd) returns Str
    proc err(self: Cmd) returns Str
    func close(self: Cmd)
    func delete(own self: Cmd, shallow call_free: Bool)
    func size() returns shallow USize
    func clone(self: Cmd) returns Cmd
}
#+end_src
